# Power Pages configuration inventory

Generated by `scripts/baseline-inventory.mjs` from `mirage/docs/baseline/catalog/power-pages.json` and `examples/project/baseline/site-manifest.json` (written by `examples/project/baseline/site.mjs`). One row per catalog item; fixture records are the site records that exercise it. Do not edit by hand.

Catalog items: 1014. Exercised: 852. Not exercised: 162.

## basic-form

| Catalog item | Field | Fixture records (kind: name, variation) | Visible on |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `pp.basicform.rendering.modal` Basic form rendering: modal dialog from list/subgrid | Dialog settings on the list or subgrid action (not on the basic form) | basicform: Baseline Child Insert (subgrid create dialog form); basicform: Baseline Child Read (subgrid details dialog form); basicform: Baseline Child Edit (subgrid edit dialog form); basicform: Baseline Item Lookup Create (form for create of the child's item lookup (opened from the lookup dialog)); basicform: Baseline Parent Lookup Create (form for create of the multistep parent lookup); list: Baseline Item List Modal (create/details/edit in modal dialogs (basic forms), default dialog size; the row link opens the details dialog); list: Baseline Item List Modal Large (create/details/edit in modal dialogs (basic forms), large dialog size; the row link opens the details dialog); list: Baseline Item List Modal Small (create/details/edit in modal dialogs (basic forms), small dialog size; the row link opens the details dialog) (`examples/project/baseline/site.mjs`) | /baseline/item-list-modal-large/, /baseline/item-list-modal-small/, /baseline/item-list-modal/ |
